# Step 6 — Narrative assignment report

Run: `icpm2027_e1_open_rep2` | Log: `bpic2019` | Taxonomy mode: `open` | Assignment model: `gemini/gemini-3.5-flash-lite`

11973 variants, 251734 cases total.

## Straightforward Procurement & Invoice Clearing (`straightforward_procurement`)

Standard, high-frequency procurement sequences with minimal activity steps and clean resolution paths ending in invoice clearing (e.g., V0001, V0002, V0005).

**Taxonomy-derivation rationale (Step 5):** These variants represent the backbone of the process volume, showing standard execution times and no anomalous rework or length profiles.

**Goal-model linkage:** (no goal model)

**Coverage:** macro 939/11973 variants (7.8%) · micro 167316/251734 cases (66.5%)

**Cohesion — structural (control-flow proximity):** intra-category mean distance 6.09, nearest other category `short_or_cancelled_orders` at mean distance 7.51

**Cohesion — profile (duration/outcome/rework):** intra-category mean distance 0.333, nearest other category `srm_integration_failures` at mean distance 0.487

## Short-Cycle or Early Deletion Orders (`short_or_cancelled_orders`)

Short tracing lengths characterized by immediate completion or quick cancellation/deletion right after purchase order creation without full lifecycle progression (e.g., V0009, V0014, V0024).

**Taxonomy-derivation rationale (Step 5):** Grounded in samples that terminate early or exhibit low trace lengths representing quick aborts or simple order generation.

**Goal-model linkage:** (no goal model)

**Coverage:** macro 240/11973 variants (2.0%) · micro 17882/251734 cases (7.1%)

**Cohesion — structural (control-flow proximity):** intra-category mean distance 5.67, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 7.51

**Cohesion — profile (duration/outcome/rework):** intra-category mean distance 0.497, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 0.539

## Service Entry Sheet & Goods Receipt Rework (`service_entry_and_receipt_rework`)

Low duration variants characterized by repeated Service Entry Sheets and Goods Receipt recordings executed instantaneously by batch resources (e.g., V0162, V0244, V0271).

**Taxonomy-derivation rationale (Step 5):** Exhibits distinctive duplicate patterns of service and receipt actions completed with zero median duration.

**Goal-model linkage:** (no goal model)

**Coverage:** macro 3016/11973 variants (25.2%) · micro 6617/251734 cases (2.6%)

**Cohesion — structural (control-flow proximity):** intra-category mean distance 83.84, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 67.85

**Cohesion — profile (duration/outcome/rework):** intra-category mean distance 0.374, nearest other category `extreme_length_churn` at mean distance 0.612

## SRM Integration & Transfer Failures (`srm_integration_failures`)

Complex lifecycle variants involving SRM sub-states, transfer attempts, transmission changes, and final system errors or failures (e.g., V2944, V2952, V2950).

**Taxonomy-derivation rationale (Step 5):** Unified by their inclusion of specialized SRM lifecycle states and failure outcomes.

**Goal-model linkage:** (no goal model)

**Coverage:** macro 213/11973 variants (1.8%) · micro 1257/251734 cases (0.5%)

**Cohesion — structural (control-flow proximity):** intra-category mean distance 16.78, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 17.34

**Cohesion — profile (duration/outcome/rework):** intra-category mean distance 0.510, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 0.487

## Extreme Length Process Churn (`extreme_length_churn`)

Extremely long, highly repetitive process executions containing hundreds of cyclical invoice creations, cancellations, and goods receipts spanning multiple years or extreme trace lengths (e.g., V3680, V3305, V3102, V3074, V6776).

**Taxonomy-derivation rationale (Step 5):** These represent outlier extreme cases with massive trace lengths and extensive internal rework loops.

**Goal-model linkage:** (no goal model)

**Coverage:** macro 650/11973 variants (5.4%) · micro 720/251734 cases (0.3%)

**Cohesion — structural (control-flow proximity):** intra-category mean distance 83.13, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 56.45

**Cohesion — profile (duration/outcome/rework):** intra-category mean distance 0.397, nearest other category `straightforward_procurement` at mean distance 0.496
